**AIDCF**

**AI Data Centre Facilities Assurance Framework**

**ASSESSMENT METHODOLOGY  
ENHANCED PRACTITIONER HANDBOOK**

**Version 1.1**

**  
Decision trees • 360 control-level procedures • 15 inspection checklists • evidence playbooks • CG-01 to CG-15 workbooks • synthetic completed examples • assessor competence/accreditation • AITG ingestion mappings**

Artifact ID: AIDCF-AM-001  
Basis: Existing AIDCF-009 Practitioner Handbook v1.0 only  
Author: Siva Sethumadhavan  
Status: Draft for controlled review

# Document control

| **Field**        | **Value**                                                                                                                                                       |
|------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Source boundary  | Only the structure, concepts, domains, families, controls, evidence references, gates, roles, methods and workpaper patterns already present in AIDCF-009 v1.0. |
| Control universe | 15 domains, 90 families and 360 controls.                                                                                                                       |
| Change           | Additive enhancement; v1.0 doctrine and lifecycle are preserved.                                                                                                |
| Enhancements     | Decision trees; individual procedures; inspection; evidence; gates; examples; competency/accreditation; AITG ingestion.                                         |
| Safety           | No hazardous or disruptive testing without explicit authority and competent engineering governance.                                                             |
| Assurance        | UNKNOWN remains visible; gates are non-compensating; automation cannot approve material decisions.                                                              |

## Preserved lifecycle

**Initiate -\> Profile -\> Scope -\> Tailor -\> Plan -\> Collect -\> Validate -\> Assess -\> Analyse -\> Gate -\> Find -\> Review -\> Decide -\> Report -\> Reassess**

# Part I - Detailed assessor decision trees

## DT-01 Assessment acceptance

**Decision question explicit and authorised?**

| **Node** | **Decision/action**                                                       | **Record**              |
|----------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------|
| 1        | Identify subject, scope and decision question.                            | Tree instance ID.       |
| 2        | Decision question explicit and authorised?                                | Evidence and rationale. |
| 3        | No: rework/stop. Yes: capability check.                                   | Selected branch.        |
| 4        | Evaluate UNKNOWN, critical gate and assurance ceiling.                    | Linked records.         |
| 5        | Obtain technical/independent review where material.                       | Reviewer decision.      |
| 6        | Terminate in Proceed, Rework, Escalate, UNKNOWN, Stop or Decision Review. | Outcome and version.    |

- Retain contrary evidence.

- Tree aids professional judgment; it does not approve decisions autonomously.

# Part II - Full control-level procedures for all 360 controls

**Every control position already present in AIDCF-009 is expanded below. IDs remain MCF-\[DOMAIN\]-001 to 024 in each domain.**

# GOV - Governance, Accountability and Assurance Direction

**Primary gates: CG-13; CG-14. Baseline evidence: charters, roles, policies, minutes and reviews.**

## GOV-01 Governance architecture

### MCF-GOV-001 - Design and governance

**Define authority, scope, measurable acceptance criteria, dependencies, applicable states, evidence owner and trigger.**

| **Element**         | **Assessor procedure**                                                                                               |
|---------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Objective           | Determine whether governance architecture supports the Capability Instance and Service Outcomes.                     |
| Scope               | Record subject, owner, boundary, lifecycle, states, interfaces and providers.                                        |
| Applicability       | Justify Apply or approved N/A; missing evidence is UNKNOWN.                                                          |
| Evidence            | Request charters, roles, policies, minutes and reviews; state claim, class, source, period, state and quality floor. |
| Method              | Select authorised review, interview, inspection, analytics, sampling or test proportionate to consequence.           |
| Design              | Verify approved intent, criteria, architecture alignment and decision rights.                                        |
| Implementation      | Reconcile baseline, current state and deviations.                                                                    |
| Operation           | Examine representative records, monitoring, exceptions and incidents.                                                |
| Failure/recovery    | Trace dependencies, common mode, safeguard action, residual state and recovery.                                      |
| Evidence assessment | Evaluate provenance, freshness, coverage, reliability, traceability, integrity and sufficiency.                      |
| Result              | Record lens-specific result, confidence, finding, conflict and UNKNOWN.                                              |
| Gate/review         | Evaluate CG-13; CG-14; obtain review and set reassessment trigger.                                                   |

# Part IV - Evidence collection playbooks linked to AIDCF-008

## E1 Documentary E1 Documentary

| **Field**        | **Requirement**                                                                          |
|------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Examples         | policies, standards, procedures, drawings, contracts and inventories                     |
| Evidence request | Assertion, subject, boundary, period, state, quality and sufficiency floor.              |
| Collection       | Authority, approval, version, scope, effective date and supersession.                    |
| Metadata         | Evidence ID, source, collector, custodian, classification, location, version, integrity. |
| Quality          | Provenance, freshness, coverage, reliability, traceability and integrity.                |
| Use boundary     | Design/intent; not operation alone.                                                      |
| Conflict         | Retain supporting, challenging and qualifying links separately.                          |
| UNKNOWN          | Create when mandatory type, source, integrity, coverage or method is unresolved.         |
| Review           | Collector, assessor, technical reviewer and acceptance state.                            |

## E2 Configuration E2 Configuration

| **Field**        | **Requirement**                                                                          |
|------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Examples         | settings, firmware, topology, manifests and access policy                                |
| Evidence request | Assertion, subject, boundary, period, state, quality and sufficiency floor.              |
| Collection       | Authorised source, extraction method, subject, time, privilege and baseline.             |
| Metadata         | Evidence ID, source, collector, custodian, classification, location, version, integrity. |
| Quality          | Provenance, freshness, coverage, reliability, traceability and integrity.                |
| Use boundary     | Implementation/current state.                                                            |
| Conflict         | Retain supporting, challenging and qualifying links separately.                          |
| UNKNOWN          | Create when mandatory type, source, integrity, coverage or method is unresolved.         |
| Review           | Collector, assessor, technical reviewer and acceptance state.                            |

## E3 Operational E3 Operational

| **Field**        | **Requirement**                                                                          |
|------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

| Use boundary     | Operation under represented conditions.                                                  |
| Conflict         | Retain supporting, challenging and qualifying links separately.                          |
| UNKNOWN          | Create when mandatory type, source, integrity, coverage or method is unresolved.         |
| Review           | Collector, assessor, technical reviewer and acceptance state.                            |

## EX-01 Synthetic AI training cluster - EX-01 Synthetic AI training cluster

| Control result     | Design, implementation, operation and effectiveness stay separate.                                                   |
| Failure/dependency | Path, safeguards, residual state and recovery recorded.                                                              |

| Decision           | Limited/conditional wording only; request checkpoint/restore exercise.                                               |
| Reassessment       | Trigger and supersession path recorded.                                                                              |

# Part VII - Assessor competency and accreditation criteria

| **Level** | **Role**             | **Minimum evidence**                                                    |
|-----------|----------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| AC-0      | Observer             | Induction, confidentiality, safety and method orientation.              |
| AC-1      | Associate assessor   | Supervised evidence intake and accepted workpapers.                     |
| AC-2      | Domain assessor      | Domain competence, witnessed fieldwork, evidence judgment and review.   |
| AC-3      | Lead assessor        | Cross-domain scope, integration, gates, findings and decision drafting. |
| AC-4      | Technical reviewer   | Recognised technical authority and current review experience.           |
| AC-5      | Independent reviewer | Impartial senior assurance competence and gate/decision challenge.      |
| AC-6      | Decision authority   | Governance appointment, independence and recorded delegation.           |

## Competency portfolio

- AIDCF method and evidence discipline.

- Assurance, sampling, findings and bounded decisions.

- Applicable engineering/technical domains.

- Safety and authorised testing.

- Professional scepticism, impartiality and confidentiality.

- Graph lineage, candidate assertion and review-state handling.

- Witnessed assessments and accepted workpapers.

- Defined scope, methods, assurance levels and roles.

- Suspension and reinstatement criteria.

# Part VIII - AITG graph-ingestion mappings

| **AIDCF record**   | **Graph object**   | **Minimum properties**                                         |
|--------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|
| Assessment         | Assessment         | ID; decision question; scope; version; status; owner           |
| Capability Profile | CapabilityInstance | ID; outcomes; lifecycle; baseline                              |
| Service Outcome    | ServiceOutcome     | ID; tolerance; criticality; conditions                         |
| Control Result     | ControlAssessment  | control; subject; state; method; result; confidence; review    |
| Evidence           | EvidenceObject     | ID; class; source; time; scope; integrity; status              |
| Assertion          | Assertion          | ID; proposition; subject; conditions; candidate/reviewed state |
| Finding            | Finding            | ID; criterion; condition; cause; consequence; status           |
| UNKNOWN            | UnknownRecord      | ID; subject; missing attribute; reason; consequence; ceiling   |
| Scenario           | FailureScenario    | ID; state; event; path; safeguards; residual; recovery         |
| Gate               | GateResult         | ID; applicability; evidence; status; decision effect; review   |
| Decision           | AssuranceDecision  | ID; subject; scope; status; conditions; validity; authority    |
| Review             | ReviewRecord       | ID; reviewer; independence; comments; disposition              |

## Relationships

| **Relationship**                  | **Use**                                    |
|-----------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| ASSESSES                          | Assessment to capability/control           |
| SUPPORTS / CHALLENGES / QUALIFIES | Evidence to assertion                      |
| EVALUATES                         | Control assessment to control or safeguard |
| AFFECTS                           | Finding/scenario to outcome                |
| DEPENDS_ON                        | Capability/function to entity/provider     |
| PROPAGATES_TO                     | Failure-path step to next step             |
| PROTECTED_BY                      | Function/outcome to safeguard              |
| EVIDENCES_GATE                    | Evidence package to gate                   |
| LIMITS                            | UNKNOWN/limitation to assertion/decision   |
| REVIEWED_BY                       | Object to review record                    |
| GENERATED_FROM                    | Derived object to sources                  |
| SUPERSEDES                        | New version to prior version               |
| INTEGRATION_HANDOFF               | AITG-native to AIDCF-native object         |

## Ingestion validation rules

- Every object has stable ID, source, version and state.

- Every assertion has evidence, challenge/qualification or UNKNOWN.

- Every derived object retains source and transformation.

- Every gate retains mandatory evidence and review.

- Candidate inference cannot become reviewed fact automatically.

- Connectivity does not imply reachability, authority, causality or realised effect.

- AIDCF and AITG decisions remain separate.

- Sensitive paths use classification and controlled query policy.

- Supersession preserves prior snapshots and lineage.

# Appendix A - Enhanced conformance

| **ID**      | **Validation**                                                                  |
|-------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| AM11-VAL-01 | Every applicable control has an individual procedure record.                    |
| AM11-VAL-02 | Every used decision tree retains the selected branch and rationale.             |
| AM11-VAL-03 | Every inspection records inaccessible scope and safety status.                  |
| AM11-VAL-04 | Every Evidence Object follows an applicable collection playbook.                |
| AM11-VAL-05 | Every applicable critical gate has a completed workbook and independent review. |
| AM11-VAL-06 | Synthetic examples remain segregated from real assessments.                     |
| AM11-VAL-07 | Every assessor role has an engagement-specific competence decision.             |
| AM11-VAL-08 | Every graph object preserves source, version and review state.                  |
| AM11-VAL-09 | No graph inference automatically changes a gate or decision.                    |
| AM11-VAL-10 | UNKNOWN, conflict, safety and non-compensation remain visible.                  |

**Final doctrine: additional procedural detail improves execution but cannot create authority, evidence, competence or certainty. Where the chain is incomplete, AIDCF reduces, conditions, suspends or withholds assurance.**
